## R48-1 — queued PID is not yet a proved Job-member handle

**P2, material before another dispatch; R45-4 remains open.** At [run_corrected.py:41](C:/Users/38909/Documents/github/maps/artifacts/unreal-source-next/worktree/Artifacts/support-prototype/producer-v2/r45/run_corrected.py:41), `identity` opens an unretained PID with `OpenProcess`, reads its creation time and admits it to `members`. The `NEW_PROCESS` path at line60 supplies that PID from a queued Job notification. There is no `IsProcessInJob(opened_handle, job.handle, ...)` check. [run_event.py:41](C:/Users/38909/Documents/github/maps/artifacts/unreal-source-next/worktree/Artifacts/support-prototype/producer-v2/r45/run_event.py:41) has the same gap. Holding the newly opened handle prevents later reuse; it does not establish that reuse had not already happened before that handle was opened. Counts and PID-only NEW/EXIT comparisons can still match if a delayed queued member PID now names a foreign process.

Microsoft's [Job completion-port contract](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/api/winnt/ns-winnt-jobobject_associate_completion_port) explicitly warns that returned PIDs can already be recycled unless an open handle is retained. [IsProcessInJob](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/api/jobapi/nf-jobapi-isprocessinjob) tests the process handle against a supplied exact Job; a null Job tests membership in any Job and is insufficient here. This is a source-inspected race counterexample, not an executed PID-reuse reproduction.

Required correction: before admitting any handle acquired from a queued PID, require successful `IsProcessInJob` against the still-open owned Job and a true result. On false/error, close that newly opened handle, retain the refusal and do not add a member or claim ownership success. Keep the exact handle held through accounting and cleanup. Preserve the suspended primary's separate direct-handle admission, or assign it before applying the same membership predicate; its current identity call occurs before assignment. A focused fake-API control should make a delayed NEW event open a foreign handle with a valid creation time, then prove refusal/handle close/no admitted member while the old admission passes incorrectly. Include false and API-error cases, a real-member positive, unchanged total/event/empty-Job requirements and the actual later finite owned run under a fresh lease. No test needs a broad process kill or real foreign-process termination.

The observed corrected wrapper PID is 13144. It retained primary PID63820 with creation FILETIME `134362630616756439` and descendant PID63084 with `134362630616949987`, records two total processes, two NEW/two EXIT notifications, ACTIVE_PROCESS_ZERO, empty actual Job membership, exit0 and successful handle closes in 6.6179898998 s. The initial primary was created suspended, its owned handle was retained, and actual Job membership was checked before resume, addressing the old pre-assignment execution race. Descendant PID63084's exact-Job handle membership was not checked. The later narrow CIM query succeeded with no rows for the wrapper, recorded members or their direct children. These observations establish the retained quiescent result; they neither identify an actual recycled process nor prove a leak. The corrected wrapper's `passed:true` must retain this review qualification.

### R48-2 — required final-hash/report and preflight timing is not recorded

**P2, material to the requested measurement before dispatch.** At [measure_sample512.py:283](C:/Users/38909/Documents/github/maps/artifacts/unreal-source-next/worktree/Artifacts/support-prototype/producer-v2/r45/measure_sample512.py:283), the complete final input rehash deliberately runs with cooperative checks disabled, but its duration is never added to `stages_seconds`. At line287, `elapsed_seconds` is sampled before the report's JSON serialization and guarded write at line290. The completion message repeats that earlier timestamp. The driver starts its total timer before imports but starts its named inventory timer only inside `main`, leaving import/preflight cost unnamed. At [run_sample512.py:57](C:/Users/38909/Documents/github/maps/artifacts/unreal-source-next/worktree/Artifacts/support-prototype/producer-v2/r45/run_sample512.py:57), helper import/hash, process creation and dispatch likewise have no separate preflight duration; line125 samples the wrapper elapsed time before its final receipt serialization/write at line126. The outer elapsed time covers the overall wait, but cannot recover these separate costs.

Consequently, a green run would not supply the assignment's required separately measured preflight, final hash and report costs. ### Predicate and independent decision gate

At `support_topology.py:126–143`, the new predicate tests exact determinants across the union of the executing clip labels and inherited common subject labels. When that union has no independent pair, the existing actual-binary-subject operator is added while retaining the previous subject ancestry. Already independent unions do not take that fallback. The patch changes neither the clipping operator nor its arithmetic, coordinate rows, cleanup/triangulation, exact line normalization/IDs, cache semantics, event priority or strong nonparallel intersection admission. Zero determinants still do not manufacture a point; genuinely parallel/coincident cases continue to reach the existing event refusal. It remains a candidate because a nonzero determinant alone says nothing about how far the intersection lies from the observed interpolation.

`measure_rank_one.py:110–157` first reproduces the exact old refusal and requires plain/old/new ordered eight-channel clipping rows and face statistics to agree. It then rereads the actual pinned source, checks fixture terrain and road candidates against that source, and derives budgets from source triangle attributes, actual road count, unchanged numeric piece count and the source construction-coordinate envelope. `quantization_bounds` is unchanged by the candidate. Position allowance uses its two-pack source half-ULPs and source-conditioned gamma term. Plane/UV allowance uses the source affine gradients; raw normals use the same unchanged formula with the original raw normal channels. The barycentric allowance propagates the finite XY allowance through source weight gradients plus the source-conditioned arithmetic term. None is sized from candidate intersection magnitude, measured displacement or observed residual.

`event_measurement` at lines66–88 measures the candidate's exact displacement against the fixed observed row, evaluates independently pinned source attributes at its candidate XY, and computes source barycentric weights. Its comparison against the fixed row's plane/UV/raw-normal attributes is not candidate-self-equality. Position, source attributes and parent membership each have their own predicate. Nonfinite or excessive position/attribute/weight results cannot satisfy the conjunction. The durable decision checkpoint is written at line153 before the line154–157 refusal and before control imports at line159. It contains the measured values, all individual decisions and the explicit downstream did-not-run list. If any first bound fails, control/constructor/writer/ledger stages are bypassed and the report identifies the rank route as falsified. This static review does not assign numerical values to those bounds or claim the candidate will pass them.

Conditional on that first decision, the gate requires all 31 controls with no skips. Static declaration matching found exactly the 24 retained controls plus seven distinct new class controls. New controls exercise the actual old16179 negative against preserved topology, unchanged numerical rows, the existing11207 empty-subject result, already independent ancestry/IDs, coincident and parallel rejection, multi-label dependent ancestry preservation, and generic full eight-channel numeric equality. Their limited successful-event assertion is explicitly separate from geometry acceptance; they cannot replace the decision gate. They have not run on the candidate.

The later all-event loop checks every non-parent-boundary road-labelled row that refinement would canonicalize, then records a durable checkpoint before the constructor. The actual one-parent constructor observes the expected pre-refinement piece digest/count/statistics, uses unchanged all-vertex plane/UV quantization and packing accounting, and adds raw-normal quantization. Conditional writer work compares complete actual float32 POSITION/TEXCOORD_0/NORMAL bytes and index order, then persists, rereads and decodes the SUPPPC01 ledger with ordered IDs, parent/piece closure and exact exclusions. It does not substitute generated expected IDs for actual readback. These remain conditional stages, not evidence already obtained.

### Directed clarification: prefer robust source reconstruction over another A pass

**This recommendation supersedes the preceding preference for preparing A first.** Root clarified that the authoritative invariants are the immutable original terrain/road roots and protected source attributes; the failed floating algorithm's derived intermediate pieces/counts are not source facts. Under that contract, a new source-faithful finite-domain construction is the more useful next family. An A probe that requires exact incidence of its already rounded rows offers little information gain: operation replay or composed fraction ancestry cannot imply incidence, and rounded interpolation generally does not preserve exact simultaneous membership on the actual subject and clip segments. I have not calculated whether this particular point passes an exact incidence predicate. That factual question remains unmeasured, but answering it is not a necessary prerequisite to choosing a different construction contract after the accepted rank falsification. Do not spend a repair pass building A's DAG merely to rediscover that distinction.

The proposed robust family should define clipping/classification on the independently admitted finite source domains, with robust/exact predicates for their required intersections and explicit handling of parallel/coincident boundaries. Infinite supporting-line intersections outside those domains are not automatically source events. Existing height-conflict and exterior-clearance/restoration semantics must be specified and preserved, or a separate change to those semantics must be explicitly approved. The original terrain parent/vertex identities, raw road/layer roots and source attribute fields remain authoritative. Source-derived plane/UV/raw-normal and final float32 allowances remain unchanged; none becomes a topology welding tolerance.

Derived decompositions may change. Assign new deterministic reconciled-piece ordinals before the new refinement, keep each child's original source parent, and prove complete coverage of the intended retained source region, excluded road-conflict regions and required boundaries. All original source parents must be accounted for; no parent or tiny nonzero output disappears because it is inconvenient. A different intermediate decomposition need not reproduce the rejected algorithm's244/391 pieces, rounded occurrence IDs or old failed piece counts. Those records remain protected diagnostic history. Exact authorized packing exclusions, all planned fan attempts, disjointness/coverage, finite values and written ordered output correspondence still require proof. Merely preserving signed area or matching incidence counts is insufficient; overlap can compensate for a missing region.

The following acceptance contracts need explicit replacement or rebinding, rather than silently weakened assertions:

- **Legacy numeric-equality and rank-route gates:** the plain/old/new ordered clipping-row/statistics equality, old piece counts and actual-line-pair success expectations in `measure_rank_one.py`, `test_support_rank_one.py`, the affected metadata comparison in `test_support_topology_cache.py`, and the corresponding old/new piece comparison in the Source512 driver describe preservation of the old algorithm. Keep their old-module negatives and artifacts as historical controls. Parallel/coincident geometry must not invent a crossing, but valid coincident boundaries need not fail merely because the retired identity model demanded two independent lines. ### Accepted direction and precise semantic delta

Restricting an eligible footprint to `T intersect raw-road-domain intersect {terrain height minus road height >= 0}` is source-faithful: an original parent's affine field has no source authority outside its finite face. Computing the union over admitted terrain, rather than independent extrapolated parent planes, also gives a defensible basis for eliminating internal triangulation seams. This is an explicit change from `support_surface.py:368–381`, which applies the parent's plane to the road triangle outside T. It must be recorded as the approved new region definition rather than claimed byte equivalence with the old recipe's resulting polygons.

The0.00025 m perpendicular rectangle/32-direction vertex-polygon construction and the1e-9 maximum-gap threshold are recognizable preserved policy choices. The old implementation also uses MIN_AREA1e-10, short-edge/piece filters, approximate collinearity, rounded-coordinate keys and repeated restoration emission. Exact Boolean union, exact predicates, retained tiny regions and unioned restoration deliberately replace those heuristics. Lines9–15 already acknowledge much of this; the implementation and controls must name all of those retirements, including `union_exterior`'s tolerance/rounded-key logic and `exterior_bands`' rounded vertex deduplication. Only identical exact geometry may be deduplicated in the new route, while every original layer alias/identity remains recorded. Neither union construction nor candidate deduplication may select a different road height/layer.

**R48-F1 — Make the regularized region and height gate unambiguous (contract lines9–15).** Define U from eligible positive-area regions, with lower-dimensional intersections/degenerate roads retained as witnesses rather than as independent removed sets or restoration generators. Otherwise a zero-area line or point can acquire a clearance rectangle/vertex polygon and restore positive area, despite the assertion that it removes none; deleting a line from a closed retained domain can also invent a crack. Degenerate projected roads need classification before attempting their undefined affine height solve. Nonzero arbitrarily small projected regions must remain eligible. Preserve the existing maximum-gap domain and ordering: evaluate the gate over the original T vertices for the original parent/road pair, before clipping or fragment subdivision; eligibility is maximum gap strictly greater than the pinned threshold, followed by the separate nonnegative gap half-plane. Do not move the threshold to each clipped fragment. Pin the existing binary64 policy constants and rectangle/trig operation order as the source of the admitted derived band bits. Exact evaluation may intentionally replace legacy rounded classification at a threshold; say so rather than claiming identical old floating decisions.

### Required completeness of the halo and independent oracle

**R48-F2 — Give the halo a constructive completion criterion (line11).** A proved enclosure is a requirement, but the contract currently leaves the observable closure decision implicit. Specify that every source region capable of affecting the target or the actual binary band recipe's enclosure is admitted with enough uncut source boundary to classify U on both sides of each relevant segment. The query/selection boundary must never become U's exterior. Incomplete frontier membership refuses; it cannot be treated as empty space. Distinguish real source-domain edges/holes from the query frontier. Because line11 merges collinear runs before using endpoint-dependent binary64 hypot arithmetic, the required canonical run endpoints must also be resolved; a halo-clipped subsegment is not automatically the same finite binary operand. Expand to complete those dependencies within the fixed caps or refuse. The enclosure must cover the actual generated binary vertices, not assume an ideal radius while ignoring endpoint conversion/arithmetic. Add a finite case with a union contributor or true segment endpoint beyond the initial halo so an incomplete local union is demonstrably rejected. This can be implemented locally; it does not require silently accepting a truncated global union or running the full source under the first lease.

**R48-F3 — Include output geometry in the slab critical set (lines23–25).** The source-only critical X values described in line23 are sufficient for source region membership, but not for certifying arbitrary candidate output. The coverage overlay must additionally subdivide at every relevant output-triangle vertex and every finite source/output and output/output edge-crossing X value, with vertical and coincident-overlap endpoint strata handled explicitly. Equivalently, require the full common refinement of source/restoration and output boundaries before midpoint incidence decisions. Without this explicit step, a missing or duplicated sliver wholly inside a source slab and away from its midpoint could escape a source-only midpoint test. Every refined open cell needs the prescribed multiplicity, and all critical-X strata need their stated separate checks. Place a gap/duplicate mutant away from the original source slab midpoint, not only on a sampled source boundary. The contract's word “overlay” may intend this refinement; it must be an actual acceptance requirement rather than an implementation assumption.

A separately coded exact slab/interval oracle is a meaningful independent approach to an arrangement/triangulation constructor. Shared source decoding and standard arithmetic do not collapse that independence; sharing region clipping, exterior extraction, band lists or the constructor's planned faces would. Independent band regeneration must start from the oracle's own source-derived boundary. Its positive embedding claim must include nonincident crossings, overlap/duplicate multiplicity, orientation and valid vertex neighborhoods as well as opposite edges, correct boundaries and no T-junction. Correct open-cell coverage plus complete boundary-stratum checking can support that claim; matching totals or edge counts cannot. Retain a witness that identifies the original parent/road roots and the offending exact cell/stratum.

### Attributes, packed evidence, policy and finite work

The proposed original-source affine evaluation and separation of rational coverage from actual packed embedding are appropriate. In particular, a packed exact-zero/collapsed exclusion must leave its nonzero rational piece in the accounting, and bounded coordinate error alone must not certify a new hole, crossing or broken shared interface. The existing finite attribute/quantization formulas remain limits to meet, not a derived entitlement to accommodate a new arithmetic path. At most two packing stages and existing final normal behavior must remain explicit. A path outside those formulas' covered operations refuses. New derived ordinals/counts are legitimate under this source-authoritative contract; protected original parents, layer identities, full per-piece closure, finite exclusion predicates and ordered persisted readback remain mandatory.

Policy2 values `exact-finite-source-region-arrangement-v1` and `finite-segment-split-overlap-intervals-v1` now describe the proposed family rather than disguising an old line-pair model. The stated trusted-producer boundary resolves the prior absent-DAG concern at contract level: output/source/code-digest-bound construction evidence is separate from native's actual geometry/ledger/inventory checks. Strict Source/native policy controls and deployment/snapshot rebinding still require coordinated review after accepted code. SUPPPC01 meanings/layout, source67 membership and six binding caps need no relaxation. Nothing in this contract constitutes native replay or a native whole-conformity proof.

The17/20/15/45-second containment proposal is acceptable only as a finite experiment that may refuse; completion cost remains unknown. Make the2048-segment,200000-pair,16384-node/cell and4096-bit quotas cumulative across construction and oracle phases for the declared experiment, or explicitly state narrower scopes with a finite aggregate limit. Count candidates before exact operations and cap source/halo expansion, output incidence work and intermediate rational growth as well as final stored operands. The new algorithm no longer has the old sequential subtraction loop, so identify the actual counted object/stage enforcing the retained512-fragment limit; merely repeating its old name is not enforcement. Preserve completed-parent/case checkpoints and mark later cases/mutants did-not-run after any cap/deadline. A green first experiment requires both real parents, the declared independent cases and the oracle's actual negative mutants; an early partial success is not green. No higher time or admission budget is approved by this review.

After R48-F1/F2/F3 and these explicit quota scopes are settled, the contract is a reasonable basis for Root's bounded implementation decision. Source-level reconstruction, both real-parent halos, oracle independence, mutation sensitivity and runtime remain unverified. ### R48-F4 — P1: derive the oracle halo independently before accepting coverage

At `finite_source_oracle.py:337–343`, the oracle checks only that the requested parent is included, then calls `source_regions(provider, candidate['halo_parents'], b)`. Lines349–368 search for omitted source parents only along the outline reconstructed from that candidate-supplied population. There is no independent equivalent of the full target-plus-clearance source-catalog seed query at `finite_source_prototype.py:397–400`. The oracle therefore proves coverage against a subset chosen by the constructor, not necessarily against the complete admitted source region.

An entirely omitted conflict component need not intersect any retained component's outline, so those outline-driven checks cannot discover it. The empty reconstructed-U case runs neither outline loop. A concrete contract-level counterexample class is a second original terrain domain coincident with or overlapping the target, using separate source indices and a different source height field: the target's own parent/road gate can be ineligible while the omitted parent's finite conflict footprint affects the target. A candidate declaring only the target halo and emitting its whole original triangle would be checked against that incomplete empty U. Matching attributes, cell multiplicities, face count and output digest would not reveal the missing source population. This is a static reasoning witness; no such case was executed or claimed to reproduce on the actual Shibuya parents.

**Minimum correction:** the oracle must start from its own complete source-catalog query for the target and the conservative binary-recipe clearance enclosure. It must independently complete all contributor, two-sided exterior and real maximal-run-endpoint dependencies using its own source-region/interval boundary logic. Build expected U/restoration/coverage from that derived population, not from `candidate['halo_parents']`. Validate candidate halo identities, uniqueness, bounds and completeness against the independently required population; cap or unresolved dependency must refuse. Sharing the immutable provider/index and arithmetic budget remains acceptable. Importing constructor halo/region helpers or simply copying its declared list is not. Preserve the cumulative quotas and 128-parent/per-parent-road limits; extra oracle work may truthfully refuse within them.

The existing outside-initial-halo control at `finite_source_controls.py:112–120` exercises only the constructor's `initial_only=True` refusal. It does not test an oracle presented with a false halo. Add independently authored oracle omission cases covering (1) a missing initial contributor/component, including the empty-U or coincident-domain case, and (2) an omitted contributor/real endpoint needed beyond the initial seed. Supply literal candidate geometry/halo data rather than having the same constructor fabricate the oracle's expected answer. Require a meaningful missing-source/halo refusal and retain a complete-halo positive counterpart. Freeze the revised exact control population explicitly; no original Source input may be omitted, no quota enlarged, and no early refusal counted as complete success.

### Static conclusions outside the finding

R48-F1 is represented in both algorithms: the original parent/road maximum-gap gate precedes finite intersection, positive-area footprints alone generate U, projected-degenerate roads and lower-dimensional intersections remain witnesses, and tiny nonzero regions are not dropped by the old MIN_AREA/short-edge predicates. The corrected contract names the old extrapolation, rounded classification and cleaning semantics being replaced. Both band implementations reproduce the pinned binary64 constants and operation order and check actual generated operands against the admitted enclosure. These are inspected paths; none has runtime evidence yet.

R48-F3 is represented by the full source/restoration/output edge critical set in `finite_source_oracle.py:381–422`, including output/output contacts, separate closed-X strata and open-cell multiplicity. Attribute checks independently evaluate the original parent's eight channels. Rational incidence, T-junction, boundary-cycle and vertex-fan checks are present. The away-midpoint gap/duplicate controls target the identified missing-refinement class. The oracle uses feasible half-plane vertices and vertical intervals rather than the constructor's clipping/Boolean helpers, but R48-F4 prevents an overall independence verdict until the expected source population is also independent.

The approved 512 unit is now explicit. Constructor `retained_arrangement_faces` splits finite operand edges, deduplicates identical atomic geometry and walks halfedges; oracle `arrangement_face_count` uses individual operand membership signatures and positive-length slab connectivity. Aliased identical operands do not create duplicate geometric faces, and point contacts do not join cells. Convex Boolean partition cells have an additional conservative cap and are not substituted for the arrangement denominator. Both methods and the false-count negative are unrun. No runtime correctness claim follows from this static inspection.

One Budget instance spans both actual parents, constructor/oracle work and all literal controls. Rational arithmetic routes reserve work/arithmetic and check operand/intermediate bit growth; source decoding/index population is separately pinned. The declared 2048 segments, 200000 work candidates, 16384 nodes/cells, 4096 bits, additional 200000 arithmetic reservations and other fixed quotas remain unchanged. The provider admits complete immutable original arrays rather than the failed old piece set. The driver persists each completed actual rational geometry, independently reads and hashes those bytes, then binds its source/code proof and durable stage checkpoint. A later refusal cannot be represented as all 18 completed cases. The first experiment deliberately excludes float32 packing, writer/ledger closure and publication; exact rational field equality is not a proof of their unchanged quantization budgets or final embedding.

No new material issue was found in the bounded ownership adaptation. The held Source creator `1abd7455...`, Source guard `fbcf41eb...` and physical helper `a4bda9b0...` are unchanged. Assignment-before-resume and same held identity checks, authoritative cleanup, completed-report timing counterparts and separate postexit census remain. The census's new missing-report path records available wrapper/dispatch identities and explicitly marks incomplete history; missing evidence or a failed outer exit cannot mint a positive certificate. Bounds remain 17 cooperative / 20 child / 15 cleanup / 45 outer wait, with the existing bounded outer termination waits and separate 10-second CIM operation; preflight, final hashing and receipt work are separately measured, not claimed inside a fictitious single elapsed bound. Timing and physical results for this experiment are entirely unmeasured.

**Readiness:** hold the first bounded lease pending R48-F4 correction, exact refreeze and focused static re-review. No broader product repair, source omission, cap change or new adoption scope is required by this finding. After closure, the two actual-parent/all-declared-control experiment may still refuse for complete-halo, touching-neighborhood, arithmetic, quota or deadline reasons; those outcomes must remain partial/RED with later work did-not-run. ### Why F4 is closed statically

`finite_source_oracle.py:335–353` now admits candidate halo data separately: exact integer parent/halo identities, a list or tuple, the 128 cap, no bool/coercion, no duplicates, catalog bounds and presence of the target. Missing/malformed/foreign claims cannot silently supply expected geometry. At lines356–367, `source_seed` builds the target bounds from admitted source rows, expands by the unchanged conservative two-clearance enclosure, and queries the full immutable source catalog before a claimed-U decision. Empty-U and disconnected/coincident source components therefore cannot disappear simply because the candidate omitted them.

At lines376–424, `complete_source_halo` starts from that independently found seed, derives original-pair half-plane footprints itself, and uses the oracle's interval union/exterior and finite segment/triangle/endpoint contacts plus original-index adjacency to complete relevant contributors and real maximal-run endpoints. Neither its signature nor its body accepts the candidate halo. It expands required identities until no unresolved dependency remains, or refuses under the same cap/budget/touching-neighborhood rules. Per-parent cached source footprints are keyed by independently required identities; the cache does not substitute candidate footprints or constructor geometry. The return describes that computed closure, rather than asserting completeness from the candidate's list.

`verify` at lines427–439 requires the claimed halo to contain the independent seed and completed dependency set, and generates expected restoration/coverage from the oracle-derived regions. Extra valid candidate identities are recorded separately and never choose expected U. Constructor geometry helpers are still not imported by the oracle. The provider/index and shared arithmetic budget remain the only permitted common infrastructure. This corrects the causal defect at the population boundary while preserving the independent half-plane/slab proof path reviewed previously.

The complete beyond-seed counterpart checks the same actual halo admission/seed/completion functions against a separately authored six-parent expected set and requires a strict expansion beyond the seed. Its result explicitly says `passed_source_halo_interface_only` and `output_coverage: not-claimed-by-this-control`. ### Cache semantics and finite accounting

`finite_source_prototype.py:159–173` builds a local immutable tuple of actual ordered edge/box records. It reserves cache storage before allocation, one work unit per admitted edge box, and computes each exact box with the existing `bounds` comparisons. The result is rebuilt for every boundary invocation; it is not reused across changed halo populations, parents or calls and has no rounded-coordinate cache key. The geometry inputs are immutable Fraction endpoint tuples from the existing constructor path.

`_cached_segment_contacts` at lines175–202 keeps the finite determinant, collinearity, t/u range and returned-point formulas of the archived uncached function. The broad phase uses the unchanged exact `boxes_meet` predicate on closed intervals, so only strict axis separation rejects; equality still reaches contact classification. The collinear branch replaces repeated endpoint-box construction with the equivalent singleton `(p,p)` and already computed edge boxes while preserving `(a,b,c,d)` order and duplicate multiplicity. The original uncached `segment_contacts` remains unchanged for all other call sites. No float broad phase, epsilon association, source pruning, manufactured crossing, interval extension or tiny-region filter was added.

The boundary loop still visits original lexicographic `(i,j)` pairs and uses the private cached path for every pair. Work is admitted before recording a visit and before the existing box test. Cache comparisons and any partial reservation are charged through the existing Budget arithmetic path; no budget reset or protector recalibration appears. Additional cache/stage storage and work are charged, so the optimization may consume another resource sooner. That is an allowed explicit refusal, not permission to reduce the declared source/face population. F4 source-halo discovery, exact bands/attributes, the separate512 arrangement census, the independent oracle and subsequent rational readback proof are not replaced by cache equivalence.

### Observation and failure causality

`finite_source_budget.py:29–55` adds bounded stage observation with at most512 entered stages, one work and one node/cell reservation before entry, and a100000-byte cap per serialized receipt. A denied pre-entry reservation raises before a stage is entered; the driver retains its failed constructor interval/error rather than inventing a completed receipt. Entered stages record actual monotonic start/end, completion/error, scalar fields and copied budget snapshots, including handled refusals. Receipt completion describes the measured block; it excludes the later serialization/observer publication, whose timing remains in the driver receipts. Nested stage intervals can overlap and must not be summed as disjoint time.

The observer receives immutable JSON bytes. The driver at `measure_finite_source.py:147–161` decodes them into report-owned data and immediately publishes the existing guarded checkpoint with the current timings. No geometry or setter is exposed, no returned geometry is replaced, and a callback/publication error propagates to refusal. Final reporting copies the retained serialized stage history. `last_observed_instrument_stage` is distinct from `last_completed_stage`, so observing a refused inner block cannot advance the completed-source/geometry/control stage.

The fields have appropriately bounded meanings: source-parent/road counts are attempts started; cache counts increase only after a box completes; bounds comparison counters are actual arithmetic reservations, including a failed operation after admission, not alleged completed comparisons. Pair receipts retain the actual call/halo round/i/j/state, admitted visits, completed contact/split pairs, completed strict-box rejections and narrow phases started. A partial pair does not increment completed-pairs. If an inner cache/pair stage refuses, its own receipt preserves the specific progress even though the surrounding boundary stage cannot finish its aggregate update. No projected pair count or estimated saving is reported as measurement.

### Controls, launch substitutions and acceptance bound

The frozen 32-name list preserves the complete prior26 prefix in order and appends six contact classes: strict disjointness, shared endpoint, vertical crossing, parallel segments with overlapping boxes, coincident finite overlap and tiny nonzero crossing. `finite_source_controls.py:77–98` admits the actual archived uncached module through fixed path ancestry, regular-file/code cap and SHA-256 `5ec5db48b6eb03994bcb39010bd1bc7f82d4fb6a642d2b2e0ed5220d611567de` before loading it. The cache controls at line243 onward call the actual cache builder/private contact path and the archived `segment_contacts`, and require both returned lists to equal independently literal expectations, preserving order and multiplicity. Both arms share the cumulative Budget. They are meaningful authored comparators; none has executed yet.

Both actual parents and the earlier oracle/F4 controls remain mandatory. The driver uses the new cache freeze and fresh cache-prefixed outputs, requires all32 ordered named results without skips, and binds any positive actual geometry to the unchanged independent source oracle/readback requirements. A contact-control result alone cannot certify boundary construction or full source coverage. Stage observation does not turn an incomplete early parent into a passed case.

### Required preparation clarifications and causal controls

These are requirements for the next executable contract/freeze, not claims that implementation already exists or new geometry changes:

1. **Bind admission and operation ownership to actual objects.** Every canonical full/literal Provider must consume the exact named ticket before its index/adjacency/cache-container allocations and retain its admitted catalog identity and root-facade ownership. Enter a case before its existing literal/actual stage reservation and all Budget-using setup; close only after required nested stage observation is accounted for. Queries or conversions without a valid case still refuse.

2. **Define failure-safe scope entry and sealing.** The extra work/node scope-record charge and its owner must be preflighted without leaving an unowned batch increment if the other reservation or entry fails. Existing stage entry uses sequential individual reservations; accepted earlier reservations on a later stage-entry refusal remain real charges to the active case. They may not be rolled back or reassigned. A failed case, failed observer/publication or failed close must preserve its charged totals and incomplete outcome and prevent a positive final certificate. Test root=sum(closed+active), sealed monotonic records, no duplicate/reopen/nesting and rejected-reservation immutability independently of candidate snapshots. The whole-batch512-stage limit must not renew at case boundaries or silently omit retained nested stages.

3. **Exercise the actual accounting implementation against independent traces.** The separate at-most12-probe/64-event/32KiB arm must call the candidate facade/admission/conservation paths, not only an independently rewritten simulator that agrees with its own expected totals. Simulated scope identities and requested accounting amounts are literal test data. Their large nominal reservations must be kept distinct from the real protector's actual trace-processing work, whose one cumulative current-limit parent and original deadline cannot reset per probe. Freeze literal expected accepted/rejected transitions, local/root/sealed totals, ticket identities, deadline identity and refusal class before deriving any candidate results. Include genuinely causal mutations for every listed guard, including both-reset, root-only/child-only, duplicate nested charge, renewed deadline, absent/duplicate catalog, incorrect aggregate declaration and old RED mutation. A quota/deadline refusal before a mutant reaches its intended guard is not that mutant's pass. Preserve the stated mathematical redundancy of the batch cap under correctly admitted children; do not claim an ordinary trace independently hits that branch.

These clarify how to implement the existing proposal faithfully; they require no alternate geometry algorithm, weakened32 definitions or enlarged probe bounds. The literal32 action statements, ordering, fixtures, exact expected-error text, actual-parent readback bindings and old provenance remain protected. The new small arm proves only ledger behavior within its literal traces. Its success cannot consume, replace or certify a canonical case.

### R48-T1 / P1 — Compact checkpoint hashes cannot recover completed full proof evidence

At measure_finite_source_timed.py:187-205, the persisted checkpoint contains counts, latest digests, proof-prefix names and bounded latest state. At lines228-237 and253-260, complete stage/control data is appended to in-memory result/ledger structures before publishing that summary. CheckpointLedger stores stage references and full control byte payloads only in RAM at finite_source_timed_evidence.py:8-10,13-26; summary at lines34-39 contains no full record payloads. The complete history first reaches a durable artifact through the final report at driver lines331-357. The phase stdout lines at205 contain only a phase name. No other inspected output records the full stage/control history as it completes.

Consequently, after several successful checkpoint acknowledgements, a later report serialization/write failure, finalization deadline or hard child exit can leave only hashes/prefix names for the completed proof. These cannot reconstruct the original receipts or control payloads. The final report can also refuse its32M evidence cap while earlier compact checkpoints have succeeded. Immutable RAM and a complete report on the successful path do not satisfy the retained requirement for durable completed evidence after later refusal. The ledger's publication-poison guard stops further admission but does not recover those lost payloads. Existing small-arm ledger mutants exercise RAM identity and a throwing writer; they do not distinguish a writer retaining only the summary from one durably retaining the full records.

Minimum correction contract: make every acknowledged complete stage/control payload recoverable from task-owned durable bytes before advancing the compact durable prefix. Preserve the original immutable payload bytes or an explicitly lossless encoding, exact type/ordinal/order/count/digest correspondence, full proof details, and the distinction between proof completion and accepted case time. A failed or partial new write must retain the prior valid committed prefix and poison further acceptance; a damaged tail cannot become a completed record. The compact checkpoint must bind that durable prefix, and final report admission must reconcile it with the complete in-memory history. Keep all existing per-record, total evidence, stage/control, time and resource bounds; do not defer mandatory proof data until the final report or substitute hashes for payloads.

A bounded append record stream in the already-owned finite-source-timed-stdout.log is a possible correction within the current17-output population: reserve and cap bytes before append, flush the complete framed record and synchronize the actual owned file before acknowledging its prefix, retain incomplete-tail refusal semantics, and bind/read back that exact prefix. This is a contract-level option for Root/worker choice, not implementation or permission to enlarge the output population. The small arm can retain its existing16 names; its compact-ledger class needs an independent literal recovery/omitted-payload control that reaches the actual persistence path within the existing12/64/32768 bounds. If a separate journal is chosen instead, its exact new filename, owner/phase, freshness, count, byte cap and recovery/admission contract must be explicitly proposed to Root before adding it. No such extra output is authorized by this review.

Under Root's explicit narrow authorization, this reviewer independently repeated exactly the same frozen24 arithmetic actions on that one saved packet using isolated Python stdlib only. Four canonical ASCII parses were admitted by the1024-digit/4096-bit preconversion bound. Every operation was admitted before execution and its result width checked afterward, including the two gcds; no Fraction/candidate import, unscaled cross product, extra gcd, new sample, geometry execution or population calculation occurred. All24 resulting decimal integers and every preflight/actual width matched the author's transcript exactly. The reviewer did not convert the diagnostic into a control golden or benchmark. The author's0.0004558s duration remains the author's isolated observation, not a reviewer Source performance measurement.

The repeated calculation gives g=gcd(da,db) of2060 bits, a=da/g=896 and b=db/g=1. The scaled products have2073 bits each under admitted bounds2074/2073. Their difference t has2074 bits under bound2075. The second gcd h=gcd(t,g) has53 bits. Reduced numerator/denominator widths are2021/2017; the result is negative. The largest computed integer intermediate is2074 bits, and the original predictor recomputes to4134. All operation bounds remain within4096. Algebraically, da=g*a and db=g*b make the difference t/(g*a*b); division of t and its denominator by h yields `(t/h)/(a*(db/h))`. Because the input Fractions are admitted reduced with positive denominators and a,b are coprime, t is coprime to a and b, so h removes the remaining common factor. The identity preserves exact value/sign and canonical reduction without constructing the rejected unscaled product.

This establishes that cancellation can evaluate this particular exact difference within the existing bit cap. It does not establish the geometric provenance of the shared factor, suitability/cost of a future primitive, its handling of every operand class, remaining Source geometry or either parent's/full32 fit. The proposed twelve classes and symbolic small-rational expectations are a future plan, not frozen executable fixtures or observed controls. Exact selector behavior, scalar/temporary/result records, operation/guard/gcd accounting, immutable-result construction and named causal negative actions still require an authorized contract and independent review before implementation. The legacy guard, old RED history and all protected caps remain unchanged.

## R48 strict bbox necessary-condition proof: nonzero area suffices (2026-10-13 PDT)

Independent reviewer: `/root/source_dom_review`. The Root challenge is valid and corrects the preceding extra eligibility restriction: convexity is unnecessary for this specific all-closed-edge predicate's STRICT bbox-outside rejection. A complete finite ordered closed ring with exact nonzero signed area, bound to the owned immutable geometry, is sufficient. The preceding demand for an additional convexity proof/scan is superseded. This does not introduce a general point-in-polygon classifier, accept bbox survivors, change the narrow predicate, or predict any cost/fit. All other before-code ownership/accounting/control/authority requirements remain.

The actual census_cross is (b.x-a.x)*(p.y-a.y) minus (b.y-a.y)*(p.x-a.x), followed by exact census_sub; contains requires its sign>=0 for every cyclic edge, including last to first. Translate p to the origin and write vi=(xi,yi). Then census_cross(vi,vi+1,0)=xi*yi+1-yi*xi+1. If p.x is strictly outside the complete vertex x range, every translated xi has the same nonzero sign, so xi*xi+1>0. A nonnegative cross therefore forces yi+1/xi+1>=yi/xi. Around the closed cycle all slopes must be equal. Every translated vertex lies on the same line through p, so every cyclic cross is zero and the shoelace sum is zero. Translation preserves that sum because the linear offset terms telescope on a closed ring. This contradicts exact nonzero signed area. Consequently at least one original edge test is negative and the unchanged all-edge verdict is false.

For strict y-outside, every translated yi has the same nonzero sign. The cross equals yi*yi+1*(xi/yi-xi+1/yi+1), so nonnegative tests force cyclic nonincreasing x/y ratios; again all ratios are equal and area is zero, giving the same contradiction. This proof does not need convexity, simplicity or positive winding, and still applies to a concave or self-intersecting ring with nonzero algebraic area. It is only a necessary-condition proof for the existing half-plane conjunction. A bbox survivor continues through every originally required narrow test in the original order; bbox equality, interior holes and generic polygon membership are not reinterpreted. The slope divisions are proof notation only. The implementation needs only the proposed exact guarded extrema/comparisons and must not add Fraction divisions to establish this lemma.

The nonzero-area requirement is real. A closed collinear ring at x=1,2,3 on y=0 and p=(4,0) has every cross zero, so the original conjunction is true despite strict x-outside. An empty operand also retains all()=true. Zero-area, empty or otherwise unproved operands must therefore keep the original fallback; a generic bbox false for them would change behavior. Duplicate vertices do not invalidate the nonzero-area argument because all cyclic comparisons still close, but their full order/coordinates must remain bound.

I rechecked the pinned prototype 32,395 bytes / `51ef508c576de5dea09d75a709a88d87babae53d680072a1b7811648814cdf6e`. Its area() computes the exact closed shoelace sum through the existing q operations. positive() cleans the complete ring, measures that sum for length>=3, returns empty when it is zero and otherwise preserves/reverses winding. Thus a nonempty returned positive() ring has the required exact nonzero-area provenance for those same coordinates. The actual caller obtains target from positive() and refuses an empty target; stored footprint finite values come from positive() and are stored only when nonempty; band rectangles come from positive() and refuse emptiness; round band polygons also come from positive() but may append an empty result. That last possibility needs the unchanged empty fallback, not a new convexity check. No geometric computation was rerun for this inspection.

The concrete before-code contract must tie that provenance to the complete privately owned immutable snapshot and invocation/family/occurrence/quota scope. A nonzero-area label from different coordinates, or an original mutable list changed after the proof, is insufficient. Reuse the actual already established nonzero-area provenance when its exact geometry is bound; do not substitute identity-only trust of mutable foreign geometry. The once-bound snapshot route and exact owner/scope identity checks from the preceding review still apply, with fully charged construction/lookup/source guards and no per-lookup full coordinate scan when immutable binding suffices. This correction removes unnecessary convexity work, not the real cache/guard allocation cost or the original quota/4096-bit/time facade. The isolated candidate's frozen contract must explicitly use this corrected eligibility condition while leaving the old proposal/review history intact.

This was one bounded saved-code and mathematical-contract review. No candidate import, Fraction/arithmetic/geometry execution, new fixture/control, source change, profiler, Job, CIM, ENV mutation, compiler, Engine, gate or lease ran. Only this authored correction was appended, preserving all incoming468,420 bytes / `aef5201ebde409520e62be4053989105f7a82753f1c15d15c56aaef93dd58688`. No source/input/proposal/raw result/plan/index changed and no persistent process or temporary artifact was created. No mathematical hold remains under the corrected nonzero-area condition. Root may assign the concrete contract/candidate author; semantic and original32/two-parent acceptance remain UNRUN requirements. Scope released promptly to Root.
